Motivation: SQD方法因依赖量子计算机采样构建子空间，存在根本性采样问题——某些对目标精度至关重要的基态被采到的概率极低，限制了方法的实际应用效果。

Method: 将SQD与振幅放大(AA)相结合，利用AA顺序性地降低已测量比特串的出现概率，从而提高观测到新比特串的可能性。

Result: 在代数衰减和指数衰减模型分布下，总查询复杂度降低超过100倍；对指数衰减情况获得二次加速优势。在实际分子评估中，SQD-AA总T门数最低，电路深度比iQPE浅3-4个数量级，总运行时间比SQD节省2个数量级。

Conclusion: SQD-AA在早期容错量子计算中具有显著优势：相比iQPE，电路深度大幅降低，使原本不可行的计算变得可行；相比SQD，总运行时间大幅减少，有望在iQPE电路过深而SQD采样效率不足的中间区域发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Recently, sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) has emerged as a promising approach to compute ground and excited states of problem Hamiltonians.This method classically diagonalizes a Hamiltonian in a subspace that is spanned by samples obtained from a quantum computer. However, by its nature, SQD suffers from a fundamental sampling problem, as some basis states that are required for a targeted accuracy may only be sampled extremely rarely. To alleviate this limitation, we introduce the SQD-AA algorithm that combines SQD with amplitude amplification (AA). SQD-AA uses AA to sequentially reduce probabilities of already measured bitstrings, thus making the observation of new ones more likely. We observe a reduction in the total query complexity of more than a factor 100 for algebraically and exponentially decaying model distributions, and analytically show a quadratic advantage for the latter. Moreover, we evaluate real molecules in an early fault-tolerant scenario and compare SQD-AA to SQD and iterative quantum phase estimation (iQPE). For all considered examples, we observe the lowest total number of T-gates for SQD-AA while only requiring circuits that are 3-4 orders of magnitude shallower than those needed for iQPE. Given this substantial reduction in circuit depth compared to iQPE while saving 2 orders of magnitude in total runtime compared to SQD, we expect a significant regime in early fault-tolerance where SQD-AA runs feasibly, but iQPE circuits are too deep to execute confidently.

</details>


### [100] [Construction of Quantum Rank-Metric Codes Using Hermitian Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02571)
*Ryota Nizuka,Ryutaroh Matsumoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对堆叠量子存储器中量子秩度码的构造问题，提出了一种基于经典线性码辛自正交性的量子秩度码框架，并利用经典Gabidulin码在有限域扩展度为偶数时的厄米自正交性，结合Matsumoto和Uyematsu的构造方法，实现了量子Gabidulin码的新构造。该方法将最小秩距与物理量子比特数的比率提升约一倍，同时保持码率不变，并消除了传统方法对存储单元和层数为奇数的限制，适用于单元和层数为偶数的存储器，提高了纠错能力和设计自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于CSS构造的量子Gabidulin码存在代数约束，严重限制了堆叠量子存储器的布局——仅适用于奇数长度的正方形结构，这限制了设计自由度和纠错性能。

Method: 首先提出从经典线性码通过辛自正交性构造量子秩度码的通用框架；然后利用经典Gabidulin码在有限域扩展度为偶数时存在的自对偶基实现厄米自正交性，结合Matsumoto和Uyematsu基于厄米正交性的量子码构造方法，设计新的量子Gabidulin码。

Result: 新方法在保持码率不变的情况下，将最小秩距与物理量子比特数的比率提升约一倍；消除了传统方法对存储单元和层数必须为奇数的限制，支持偶数单元和层数的存储器布局。

Conclusion: 该构造提升了堆叠量子存储器的相对纠错能力，同时增加了设计自由度，为堆叠量子存储架构提供了更灵活的量子秩度码设计方案。

Abstract: Stacked quantum memory is an architecture in which multiple layers of qubits are stacked. Quantum rank-metric codes are effective for error correction in stacked quantum memories. However, the previously proposed quantum Gabidulin codes based on the CSS construction had a problem: due to algebraic constraints, the applicable memory layouts were strictly limited to square shapes of odd length. In this paper, we first propose a framework for constructing quantum rank-metric codes from classical linear codes with symplectic self-orthogonality. Building upon this, we propose a new construction method for quantum Gabidulin codes by combining the Hermitian self-orthogonality of classical Gabidulin codes--utilizing the self-dual basis that exists when the extension degree of the finite field is even--with the quantum code construction method using Hermitian orthogonality by Matsumoto and Uyematsu. The proposed method succeeds in approximately doubling the ratio of the minimum rank distance to the number of physical qubits while maintaining the code rate. Furthermore, it eliminates the restriction of the conventional method that requires the number of cells and layers of the stacked memory to be odd, realizing the construction of quantum rank-metric codes applicable to memories with an even number of cells and layers. This construction improves the relative error correction capability of the stacked quantum memory architecture and increases the degree of freedom in design while preserving the code rate.

</details>


### [101] [Comment on `On computing quantum waves exactly from classical action'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02621)
*Gabor Vattay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出 Lohmiller & Slotine 声称仅用经典最小作用量和流体密度就能精确求解薛定谔方程，但本文证明其推导存在根本性错误：忽略了概率密度振幅的空间导数，从而无意中省略了量子势项，导致其等价关系实际上只是标准半经典近似。文中所有示例要么属于量子势恒为零的特例，要么通过初始条件导入了量子本征函数掩盖了错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出 Lohmiller & Slotine 近期论文中声称"仅用经典力学就能精确求解薛定谔方程"这一论断存在数学错误，澄清其实际只是半经典近似，而非真正的精确等价。

Method: 通过数学推导分析，指出原文忽略了概率密度振幅的空间导数项，即遗漏了 Madelung 最早识别、Bohm 后来强调的量子势项；并通过逐一检验原文的示例，证明它们要么属于量子势自然为零的几何特例，要么通过初始条件暗中引入了量子本征函数。

Result: Lohmiller & Slotine 的推导并非精确等价，而是标准半经典近似；其提供的所有示例无法支撑其结论。

Conclusion: 声称仅用经典概念就能精确求解薛定谔方程的主张是不成立的，其推导实质上是半经典近似。

Abstract: A recent article by Lohmiller \& Slotine (Proc.\ R.\ Soc.\ A \textbf{482}: 20250413) claims that the Schrödinger equation can be solved exactly using only classical least action and classical fluid density, asserting that this formulation avoids semiclassical approximations. We show that their mathematical derivation contains a foundational error. By neglecting the spatial derivatives of the probability density amplitude, the authors inadvertently omit the quantum potential -- the term originally identified by Madelung and later emphasised by Bohm. Consequently, their proposed equivalence is not exact but rather constitutes the standard semiclassical approximation. We further demonstrate that each of the paper's illustrative examples either belongs to a class where the quantum potential vanishes identically due to the geometry of the problem, or recovers the correct quantum result by importing quantum eigenfunctions through the initial conditions, thereby concealing the error.

</details>


### [102] [Temporal State Tomography via Quantum Snapshotting the Temporal Quasiprobabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02655)
*Zhian Jia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出时间状态层析（TST）新范式，基于时间准概率分布（TQD）在信息完备设置下重构多时间量子过程，统一了密度算符和量子通道的重构，并推导了样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子层析方法分别处理态和通道重构，缺乏统一的多时间过程描述框架。作者希望引入时间准概率分布，建立一种能够同时重构密度算符和量子通道的通用方案。

Method: 基于时间准概率分布（TQD），在信息完备设置下定义时间状态；利用固定量子仪器组测量结果进行经典后处理获取TQD；采用时间布洛赫表示重构时间状态，并分析样本复杂度。

Result: 证明了任何TQD均可通过固定量子仪器组的测量结果经经典后处理获得；建立了时间状态与TQD之间的一一对应关系；推导了TST的样本复杂度，量化了统计效率。

Conclusion: TST为多时间量子过程重构提供了统一、操作可行的框架，兼具统计效率，有望在量子信息科学中广泛应用。

Abstract: Quantum tomography is a cornerstone of quantum information science, enabling the reconstruction of states and channels from experimental data. Here we introduce a new paradigm, temporal state tomography (TST), for reconstructing quantum processes across multiple times. Our approach is based on temporal quasiprobability distributions (TQDs), which, in the informationally complete setting, provide a complete description of multi-time quantum processes and uniquely determine temporal states. We formulate TST as a unified framework for reconstructing both density operators and quantum channels within a single scheme. We show that any TQD can be obtained via classical post-processing of measurement outcomes generated by a fixed set of quantum instruments, thereby establishing a direct operational route to accessing TQDs experimentally. For informationally complete TQDs, the associated temporal state can be reconstructed via a temporal Bloch-type representation. Leveraging this correspondence, we derive the sample complexity of TST, thereby quantifying its statistical efficiency.

</details>


### [103] [Learning Temporal Patterns in Financial Time Series: A Comparative Study of Quantum LSTM and Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02656)
*Danyal Maheshwari,Gerhard Hellstern,Martin Zaefferer,Martin Braun,Tanja Döhler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探索了量子-经典混合架构（QLSTM和QRC）在金融时间序列预测中的应用，通过幅度编码将滞后期数据映射到量子态，实验表明在多元时间序列中量子增强架构能适度超越经典基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算与经典混合架构在金融时间序列预测中的潜力，验证量子增强模型（QLSTM和QRC）是否能在实际金融数据上达到或超越经典模型（LSTM、RC）的性能。

Method: 采用QLSTM和QRC两种量子-经典混合架构，使用幅度编码将单变量/多变量滞后观测值编码为量子态，通过参数化量子电路实现循环动态和储层计算，经典优化器训练读出层和变分电路参数。

Result: 在单变量设置中量子模型与经典基线持平；在具有相关输入的多变量设置中，量子增强架构能适度超越经典模型，其中表达能力强的编码方式收益最明显。

Conclusion: 选择合适的滞后结构和幅度编码后，量子-经典混合架构在金融时间序列预测中具有竞争力，尤其在多变量相关输入场景下展现出优势，为量子机器学习在金融领域的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This study explores quantum and classical hybrid architectures for financial time-series fore casting, focusing on Quantum Long Short-Term Memory (QLSTM) networks and Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC), using univariate and multivariate lag structures on real financial data. We assess how lag embeddings affect predictive accuracy and robustness. Data are en coded into quantum states via amplitude encoding, enabling efficient representation of normalized lagged observations under realistic qubit constraints. The recurrent dynamics of QLSTM and the reservoir of QRC are implemented as parameterized quantum circuits, while classical optimizers train the readout and, where applicable, variational circuit parameters. We benchmark quantum models against classical LSTM and reservoir computing using common error like metrics. Our results show that, with suitable lag selection and amplitude encoding, quantum-enhanced archi tectures match classical baselines in univariate settings and can modestly outperform them in multivariate regimes with correlated inputs, where expressive encodings are most beneficial.

</details>


### [104] [Phase-space measurements and decoherence for angular momentum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02696)
*Dorje C. Brody,Eva-Maria Graefe,Rishindra Melanathuru*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子系统角动量（自旋）三个独立分量的环境监测模型，比较了Lindblad方程和SU(2)相干态POVM两种方式，发现两种模型虽可交换但特征值不同，且对于角动量系统，密度矩阵退相干与准概率分布正性所表征的经典化不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索角动量（自旋）系统在无偏好取向的环境监测下的退相干模型，比较连续动力学Lindblad方法与迭代测量POVM方法之间的差异，并检验不同经典性判据的等价性。

Method: 采用两种模型：（1）三个独立角动量算符生成的Lindblad主方程；（2）基于SU(2)相干态的正算子值测度（POVM）的迭代球面"相空间"测量。分析两种超算符的可交换性及特征值，并比较密度矩阵退相干速率与准概率分布正性两种经典性表征。

Result: 两种模型对应的超算符可交换，但特征值不同；两种模型的动力学行为不等价；对于角动量系统，退相干速率与准概率分布正性所表征的经典性不等价。

Conclusion: 对于角动量系统，平直相空间中等价的情景不再成立，两种退相干模型存在微妙差异，且不同的经典性判据（退相干 vs. 准概率正性）并不等价。

Abstract: The monitoring of the three independent components of the angular momentum (or spin) of a quantum system by its environment that does not isolate any preferred orientation is modelled in two different ways. One describes the dynamics by the Lindblad equation generated by three independent angular momentum operators. The other uses iterated measurements of the ``phase-space'' point on the sphere in terms of the positive operator-valued measure generated by SU(2) coherent states. In contrast to the equivalent scenario on a flat phase space, these two models give rise to subtle differences. Specifically, it is shown that the two super-operators corresponding to the two decoherence models for angular momentum systems are commutative, but their eigenvalues are different. Hence although both models give rise to phase-space decoherence, their dynamical behaviours are not equivalent. In either model, we find that the characterisation of classicality as represented by the decay rates of the elements of the density matrix (i.e. decoherence) and that as represented by the positivity of the quasiprobability distribution are not equivalent for angular momentum systems.

</details>


### [105] [Automated experimental design for high-probability entanglement generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02721)
*Carlos Ruiz-Gonzalez,Mario Krenn,Xuemei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动设计算法，用于优化量子实验中纠缠光子对的保真度和成功概率，同时考虑高阶多对发射事件，并展示了在贝尔态、W态和NOON态等广泛使用的量子态上的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上， probabilistic 光子对源在低增益（微扰）近似下处理，仅保留单对项而忽略高阶多对发射事件。这导致追求保真度时成功概率极低，但这种权衡常被忽略，因此需要一种同时优化保真度和成功概率的方法。

Method: 开发了一种自动设计算法，探索不同设计拓扑结构并考虑硬件约束，优化源参数以抑制或利用高阶多对发射项。

Result: 该算法在贝尔态、W态和NOON态等量子态上优于此前方案，实现了保真度和成功概率的双重提升。

Conclusion: 该工作为更高效的光子技术铺平了道路，表明高阶多对发射事件可以被利用而非简单地忽略或抑制。

Abstract: Entangled photons are widely used in quantum technologies. Many photonic experiments generate them with probabilistic photon-pair sources that can be modeled as squeeze operators. In practice, these sources are usually treated in the low-gain (perturbative) regime, keeping only the leading single-pair term and neglecting higher-order multi-pair emission events. In pursuit of fidelity, the probability of successful entanglement generation can become extremely small, a tradeoff often ignored. Here we develop an automated design algorithm for quantum experiments to optimize both fidelity and success probability while accounting for higher-order multi-pair emissions. Our discovery algorithm explores different design topologies subject to varying hardware constraints. It optimizes the source parameters to reduce undesired higher-order terms or even benefit from them. The experiments presented outperform previous proposals for widely used states, including heralded Bell states, W states, and NOON states, paving the way for more efficient photonic technologies.

</details>


### [106] [Distributed Quantum Circuit Optimisation: Evaluating Global and Local encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02727)
*Maria Gragera Garces,Majid Haghparast*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在分布式量子架构下，量子电路优化与电路分区之间的相互作用，比较了三种编译策略（全局优化、局部优化和混合方法）对分布式量子工作负载的影响。结果表明，电路优化并非 uniformly 有利于分布式执行：全局优化最小化计算资源和编译开销，局部优化可降低通信成本，混合策略能同时减少计算和通信开销，但编译时间显著增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式量子架构开始出现，理解量子电路优化与电路分区之间的相互作用变得越来越重要。本研究旨在探索电路优化如何在系统级权衡下影响分布式量子工作负载。

Method: 使用基于远程门（telegate）的分区方法，在大量量子算法基准测试上比较了三种编译策略（全局优化、局部优化和混合方法），从门数量、电路深度、非局部门数量和编译开销四个方面评估了生成的分布式电路，从而近似计算、通信和经典预处理成本。

Result: 电路优化并不统一地有利于分布式执行。全局优化最小化了计算资源和编译开销；局部优化可以降低通信成本，尽管它并未显式地考虑通信；混合策略能够同时减少计算和通信开销，但代价是编译时间显著增加。

Conclusion: 不同的编译策略在分布式量子计算中存在不同的权衡：全局优化适合计算资源受限的场景，局部优化有助于降低通信成本，混合策略可兼顾两者但需要承受高昂的编译开销。这为未来分布式量子架构的编译优化提供了指导。

Abstract: As distributed quantum architectures begin to emerge, understanding the interaction between quantum circuit optimisation and circuit partitioning becomes increasingly important. In this work, we study how circuit optimisation influences distributed quantum workloads under system-level trade-offs. We compare three compilation strategies (global optimisation, local optimisation, and a hybrid approach) across a large benchmark suite of quantum algorithms. Using telegate-based partitioning, we evaluate the resulting distributed circuits in terms of gate counts, circuit depth, the number of induced non-local gates, and compilation overhead, thereby approximating computational, communication, and classical preprocessing costs. Our results show that circuit optimisation does not uniformly benefit distributed execution. Global optimisation minimises computational resources and achieves the lowest compilation overhead. Local optimisation can reduce communication cost even though it is not explicitly communication-aware. The hybrid strategy can simultaneously reduce both computational and communication overhead, but at the expense of significantly increased compilation time.

</details>


### [107] [Bound States and Resonance Analysis of One-Dimensional Relativistic Parity-Symmetric Two Point Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02733)
*Carlos A. Bonin,Manuel Gadella,José T. Lunardi,Luiz A. Manzoni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维狄拉克方程在关于原点对称的两个点上的最一般相对论性接触相互作用，使用分布法（等价于自伴延拓方法）来描述相互作用，每个点上的相互作用由四个具有明确物理意义的参数决定，并重点研究了该模型在奇偶性变换下的散射和约束性质，包括临界/超临界态、束缚态、约束和散射共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和描述一维狄拉克方程在对称双点上的最一般相对论性接触相互作用，并研究其散射和约束特性。

Method: 使用分布法（distributional method）来描述相互作用，该方法等价于通过对称算子的自伴延拓来定义接触相互作用的标准方法。每个点上的相互作用由四个具有明确物理意义的参数描述。

Result: 研究了在宇称变换下偶/奇相互作用的性质；发现了某些特定相互作用中存在的临界态、超临界态、束缚态、约束态和散射共振现象。

Conclusion: 该模型能够描述具有清晰物理参数的最一般相对论性双点接触相互作用，并展示了丰富的量子现象（临界/超临界态、束缚态、约束和散射共振），对理解一维狄拉克方程中的点相互作用具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We consider the one-dimensional Dirac equation with the most general relativistic contact interaction supported on two points symmetrically located with respect to the origin. In order to determine the shape of the interaction, we use a distributional method, which in the present case is equivalent to the standard method of defining contact interactions by self-adjoint extensions of symmetric operators. The interaction on each of these two points depends on four parameters, each one having a clear physical meaning. We are interested in the scattering and confining properties of this model. We focus our attention on even or odd interactions under parity transformations and investigate the existence of critical and supercritical states, bound states, confinement and scattering resonances for some particular interactions of special interest.

</details>


### [108] [Operator spreading and recoverability of local quantum Fisher information in a $U(1)$-broken spin chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02774)
*Marcin Płodzień,Jan Chwedeńczuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究XX自旋链中参数灵敏度在单点编码后的传播与局域可恢复性。通过分析三种局域计量可及性（裸单点QFI、变分扫描解码恢复的QFI、精确块QFI），揭示了算子扩展与计量可恢复性之间的区别：可积极限下灵敏度以单磁子波包传播，而U(1)对称性破缺导致双磁子散射使局域QFI在O(h²)阶出现耗散，解码QFI与块QFI之间的间隙反映了有限维压缩的本质限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注算子扩展的因果光锥，但参数灵敏度是否能局域恢复尚不明确。本文旨在区分算子扩展与计量可恢复性，探究局域计量信息可及性的层次结构。

Method: 在XX自旋链上单点编码参数，施加U(1)破缺横向场，计算三种局域计量可及性：裸单点QFI、变分扫描解码恢复的QFI、精确块QFI。解析分析可积与不可积情况下的灵敏度传播行为。

Result: 可积极限下单比特解码器可恢复完整块QFI；U(1)破缺后局域QFI一阶修正为零，耗散始于O(h²)的二阶双磁子散射；解码QFI低于精确块QFI（反映压缩限制），块QFI低于全局值（灵敏度扩散到非局域关联）。

Conclusion: 算子扩展与计量可恢复性存在本质区别，局域可及性存在精确的层次结构：裸单点QFI ≤ 解码QFI ≤ 块QFI ≤ 全局QFI，这为区分算子支持到达与计量信息局域可恢复性提供了量化框架。

Abstract: While out-of-time-order correlators establish a causal light cone for operator spreading, they do not guarantee that the parameter sensitivity carried by the operator remains locally recoverable. We examine the distinction between operator spreading and metrological recoverability for a parameter encoded in a single site of an XX spin chain subjected to a $U(1)$-breaking transverse field. We evaluate three levels of local metrological accessibility: the bare single-site quantum Fisher information (QFI), the QFI recovered by a variational sweep decoder acting on a finite spatial block, and the exact block QFI. In the integrable limit, the sensitivity propagates as a one-magnon wave packet, and a single-qubit decoder recovers the full block QFI. Breaking magnon-number conservation couples the parameter tangent state to multi-magnon sectors. We analytically demonstrate that the local QFI has no first-order correction in field strength; the leading depletion enters at $\mathcal{O}(h^2)$ through two-magnon scattering. As the field strength increases, the decoded QFI falls below the exact block QFI -- a gap reflecting a generic finite-dimensional compression limitation, as a single output qubit generically cannot capture the full QFI of a block state whose parameter dependence spans more than an effective two-dimensional subspace. The block QFI itself falls below the conserved global value, confirming that the sensitivity has spread beyond the block into non-local correlations. This operational hierarchy provides a precise quantitative distinction between the arrival of operator support and the local accessibility of metrological information.

</details>


### [109] [Entanglement cost in non-local quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02840)
*Alex May*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是对非局域量子计算（NLQC）的专著级综述，梳理了其在量子密码学、计算复杂性、通信复杂性、量子引力等领域的应用，并总结了NLQC中纠缠成本的上界与下界研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLQC作为一种无需直接接触即可实现两系统间量子操作的协议，其纠缠成本的理解与多个领域的关键问题密切相关，亟需系统性的梳理与总结。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，回顾了NLQC的协议框架（单轮通信+共享纠缠），系统整理了纠缠成本的上下界结果，并讨论了其与量子密码学、计算复杂性、通信复杂性、量子引力等方向的联系。

Result: 总结给出了NLQC纠缠成本的上界与下界结果，并概述了NLQC在多个交叉领域的应用现状。

Conclusion: NLQC的纠缠成本研究是连接量子信息论与量子引力等多个领域的关键纽带，未来仍有大量开放问题值得探索。

Abstract: This is a book-length treatment of the subject of non-local quantum computation (NLQC). NLQC is a method for implementing quantum operations that interact two systems without directly bringing the systems together. Instead, a single round of communication and shared entanglement is used. NLQC has appeared in the context of quantum cryptography, computational complexity, communication complexity, quantum gravity, and other applications. The understanding of entanglement cost in NLQC is closely tied to questions in all of these areas. We review upper and lower bounds on entanglement cost, as well as some of the applications of NLQC and its connections to other subjects.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum Tilted Loss in Variational Optimization: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02850)
*Yixian Qiu,Josep Lumbreras,Xiufan Li,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子倾斜损失（QTL），通过单参数调控重塑变分量子算法的优化景观，在放大梯度信号的同时保持全局最优，并揭示了可训练性与可估计性之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法（VQAs）在训练过程中常遭遇贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）问题，即优化景观平坦导致梯度消失，严重限制了其可训练性。现有方法虽有所缓解，但缺乏统一的理论框架来系统性地重塑优化景观。

Method: 引入量子倾斜损失（QTL），这是经典指数倾斜在算子层面的推广。通过调节单一连续参数，QTL能够在保持问题真实全局最小值的前提下放大结构化场景中的梯度信号。该框架统一了标准期望值最小化与CVaR、Gibbs公式等可调启发式方法。

Result: 理论证明了可训练性与可估计性之间的权衡：激进倾斜将优化瓶颈从景观平坦性转移到样本复杂度，即从梯度消失转变为测量采样方差。数值模拟表明，在有限样本场景下，递增倾斜调度优于固定倾斜训练。

Conclusion: QTL为VQAs提供了一种系统性的优化景观重塑工具，通过参数调控实现梯度增强与统计代价的平衡。操作瓶颈从梯度消失转向测量采样方差，为近-term量子设备的实用化训练提供了新策略。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) are leading strategies for using near-term quantum devices, with a well-studied bottleneck being their trainability. Standard expectation-value objectives with expressive circuits frequently encounter barren plateaus in the optimization landscape during training. To address this challenge, we introduce the Quantum Tilted Loss (QTL), an operator-level generalization of classical exponential tilting designed to systematically reshape the optimization landscape. By tuning a single continuous parameter, QTL can amplify gradient signals in structured settings while preserving the problem's true global minima. We provide a theoretical foundation that unifies standard expectation minimization with popular tunable heuristics, such as Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) and Gibbs formulations. Deploying this framework requires balancing the geometric benefits of a sharpened landscape against the statistical cost of estimating nonlinear gradients from finite quantum measurements. We formalize this trainability-estimability trade-off, demonstrating how aggressive tilting fundamentally shifts the optimization bottleneck from landscape flatness to sample complexity. Thus, the operational bottleneck shifts from vanishing gradients to measurement sampling variance. Finally, we exhibit through numerical simulations that ascending tilt schedules can outperform fixed-tilt training in finite-shot regimes.

</details>


### [111] [Precision hyperfine spectroscopy of an individual nuclear-spin-9/2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02857)
*J. Travesedo,Z. W. Huang,L. Mykolyshyn,N. Thill,L. Pallegoix,P. Goldner,T. Chaneliere,S. Bertaina,T. Charpentier,D. Esteve,P. Abgrall,D. Vion,J. OSullivan,E. Flurin,P. Bertet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用CaWO4晶体中的Er3+顺磁中心作为纳米级磁传感器，通过微波光子计数技术在10 mK下实现了对邻近单个93Nb核自旋的NMR谱测量，达到了赫兹级光谱分辨率。研究确定了93Nb的插入位点、相对于Er3+的位置及其完整四极张量，并发现了两种此前未被观测到的自旋哈密顿量项：Er3+自旋与93Nb核四极矩的耦合项（可能源于自旋依赖的静电相互作用），以及核十六极项（可能由电场三阶导数与93Nb核十六极矩耦合引起）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现单自旋磁共振光谱学，在单个原子或分子层面以非侵入方式获取结构和化学信息，并探索此前未知的自旋相互作用项。

Method: 以CaWO4晶体中Er3+顺磁中心作为纳米级磁传感器，在10 mK温度下通过微波光子计数技术，对邻近单个核自旋-9/2的93Nb杂质进行NMR测量。

Result: 实现了赫兹级光谱分辨率的单核自旋NMR测量，确定了93Nb的插入位点、相对位置及完整四极张量，并发现了两种新的自旋哈密顿量项（Er3+-93Nb四极耦合项和核十六极项）。

Conclusion: 单自旋磁共振光谱学能够在单个核自旋水平上揭示精细的晶体场和自旋相互作用信息，为纳米尺度的结构和化学分析提供了新手段。

Abstract: Single-spin magnetic resonance spectroscopy promises to yield structural and chemical information at the level of individual atoms or molecules, in a non-invasive way. Here, we use an Er3+ paramagnetic center in a CaWO4 crystal, detected by microwave photon counting at 10 mK, as a nanoscale magnetic sensor to measure the NMR spectrum of a proximal individual nuclear-spin-9/2 93Nb impurity with Hertz spectral resolution. From these measurements, we determine the 93Nb insertion site, its position relative to the Er3+ , and its complete quadrupolar tensor. We moreover harness the high spectral resolution of our measurements to establish the presence of two previously unobserved terms in the spin Hamiltonian. The first describes a coupling between the Er3+ spin and the 93Nb nuclear quadrupole; it possibly originates from a spin-dependent electrostatic interaction between the two systems. The second is a nuclear hexadecapolar term, and may be caused by the coupling of the electric field third derivative to the 93Nb nuclear hexadecapolar moment.

</details>


### [112] [Opportunities and challenges in scaling quantum error detection on hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02861)
*Yanis Le Fur,Ethan Egger,Hong-Ye Hu,Vincent Russo,William J. Zeng,Ryan LaRose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过重复码和三角色码在多达74个物理量子比特上的存储实验和逻辑计算，系统研究了量子错误检测作为纠错技术在真实和模拟噪声量子计算机上的性能、挑战和前景，并估算了伪阈值以绘制当前和未来量子计算机上错误检测的前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子错误检测可以产生无偏期望值并随码距增加指数收敛到无噪声结果，但作为错误缓解技术其性能研究不足。主要面临样本数随电路深度/噪声水平指数增长、经典处理随码距指数增长（尽管有例外）以及嵌入码和逻辑操作的恒定但较大的开销可能反而降低精度等问题。本文旨在清晰描绘这些机遇与挑战。

Method: 使用重复码和三角色码在真实和模拟噪声量子计算机上进行存储实验和逻辑计算基准测试，最多使用74个物理量子比特，并估计代码的伪阈值。

Result: 尽管存在样本增长和开销等挑战，但结果显示量子错误检测在硬件上具有良好的扩展前景。

Conclusion: 量子错误检测在硬件上的规模化应用具有强有力的前景，但需要权衡其样本复杂度、经典处理开销和代码嵌入成本等挑战。

Abstract: Quantum error detection can produce unbiased expectation values that exponentially converge to noiseless results as the code distance is increased. Despite this, its performance as an error mitigation technique is relatively understudied on quantum hardware because of its two main drawbacks: (i) the number of samples increases exponentially in the circuit depth/noise level, and (ii) the classical processing generally grows exponentially in the code distance, though exceptions exist. Additionally, the constant (but often large) overhead of embedding the code and logical operations on hardware can make accuracy worse instead of better. In this work, we seek to provide a clear picture of these opportunities and challenges for scaling quantum error detection on hardware. We do so by performing a detailed benchmarking study on real and simulated noisy quantum computers, using the repetition code and triangular color code for memory experiments and logical computations with up to $74$ physical qubits. In addition to these benchmarks, we estimate the pseudothreshold of codes to map the frontier of error detection on current and future quantum computers. Despite the challenges, our results show strong promise for scaling quantum error detection on hardware.

</details>


### [113] [A measure for genuine tripartite entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02876)
*Shengjun Wu,Kaichen Zhong,Jeffery Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于四组三量子比特关联期望值的实值泛函I(n₁,n₂)，将GHZ代数悖论转化为真正的三体纠缠定量见证，证明其有界性（≤2），饱和条件对应GHZ态，并推广至三量子比特系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将GHZ代数悖论从定性论证转化为定量的真正三体纠缠判定工具，实现对三量子比特纠缠的定量化区分与度量。

Method: 构造四组三量子比特关联期望值组成的实值泛函I(n₁,n₂)；证明其有界性及饱和条件；在Acín标准族上导出闭式表达式；计算W态的极值；利用Heisenberg-Weyl算符推广至三量子比特系统。

Result: I(n₁,n₂)对所有三量子比特态≤2，饱和当且仅当测量基互无偏且态局部幺正等价于GHZ态；W态上I(ẑ,ŷ)=0，最大值约1.296；诱导量取值[0,1]，仅在GHZ类上为1；推广至d维系统后d=2时退化回标准构造。

Conclusion: 该泛函是有效的设备无关GHZ型真正三体关联指标，能严格区分GHZ态与W态的三体纠缠性质。

Abstract: We introduce a single real-valued functional $I(\vec{n}_1,\vec{n}_2)$, built from four three-qubit correlation expectation values, that turns the Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) algebraic paradox into a \emph{quantitative} witness of genuine tripartite entanglement. We prove that for every three-qubit state $ρ$ and every pair of measurement directions $|I(\vec{n}_1,\vec{n}_2;ρ)|\le 2$, with the bound saturated if and only if the two measurement bases are mutually unbiased and $ρ$ is locally unitarily equivalent to the GHZ state. We obtain a closed-form expression for $I(\hat{x},\hat{y})$ on the five-parameter Acín canonical family of three-qubit pure states. For the W state we show that $I(\hat{x},\hat{y})=0$ and that $\max_{\vec{n}_1,\vec{n}_2}| I_{W}|=35/27\approx 1.296$, strictly below the GHZ value. The induced quantity ranges in $[0,1]$, equals one only on the GHZ class, and is therefore a device-independent indicator of GHZ-type genuine tripartite correlation. We also outline a generalisation of $I$ to three-qudit systems built from the Heisenberg--Weyl operators, recovering the standard qubit construction when $d=2$.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

### [114] [Model-agnostic cooling algorithms for strongly interacting fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01029)
*Henning Schlömer,Liyuan Chen,Susanne F. Yelin,Hong-Ye Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需谱信息的随机化对称性保持冷却算法，利用局部耦合算子和随机采样能级分裂将通用费米子系统驱动到低能流形，并在多种强关联费米子模型中验证了其普适冷却行为，为在可编程量子设备上制备强关联费米子态提供了通用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强相互作用费米子系统（如高温超导体）的低能态蕴含关键微观性质，但经典方法难以求解；现有量子冷却协议通常需要准粒子谱知识或自由费米子映射信息，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出一种随机化的对称性保持冷却算法，无需任何谱信息，仅通过将局部耦合算子连接到具有随机采样能级分裂的辅助自由度，驱动通用费米子系统进入低能流形。

Result: 在金属相、密度波相、配对相、超导相和相分离相等多种高温超导体相关费米子模型中，观察到普适冷却行为：单调能量弛豫、低能谱权重集中以及关联基态序的稳定化。

Conclusion: 随机化耗散冷却可作为在可编程量子设备上制备强关联费米子态的一种通用策略。

Abstract: Strongly interacting fermions underpin some of the most challenging problems in condensed matter physics, such as high-temperature superconductivity. The low-energy states of these systems encode their essential microscopic properties, yet remain largely inaccessible to classical methods. Quantum simulation offers a promising path forward, and among state-preparation strategies, engineered dissipation has emerged as a particularly compelling approach. Existing cooling protocols, however, typically rely on knowledge of the quasiparticle spectrum or mappings to free-fermion limits. In this letter, we introduce a randomized, symmetry-preserving cooling algorithm that requires no spectral information, using only local coupling operators to ancilla degrees of freedom with randomly sampled energy splittings to drive generic fermionic systems toward their low-energy manifold. We benchmark the protocol on canonical correlated fermionic models relevant to high-temperature superconductors, spanning metallic, density-wave, paired, superconducting, and phase-separated phases. Across all models, we observe universal cooling behavior: monotonic energy relaxation, concentration of spectral weight at low energies, and stabilization of correlated ground-state order. Our results establish randomized dissipative cooling as a general strategy for preparing strongly correlated fermionic states on programmable quantum devices.

</details>


### [115] [Manipulation of electromagnetic wave propagation in quantum-spin-chain medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01033)
*Taras Krokhmalskii,Taras Verkholyak,Ostap Baran,Dmytro Yaremchuk,Taras Hutak,Oleg Derzhko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维磁晶模型中电磁波的传播，通过计算色散关系k(ω)，展示了如何利用外磁场控制电磁波的传播，为更复杂的磁性介质模型提供了严格的数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一维磁晶这一简化模型中电磁波的传播特性，并验证外磁场对电磁波传播的控制能力，为数学上难以处理的更现实磁性介质模型提供严格的计算基础。

Method: 建立一维磁晶的简化模型，通过计算电磁波在该介质中的色散关系k(ω)来研究传播特性。

Result: 得到了色散关系k(ω)的具体形式，清晰展示了外磁场对电磁波传播的控制效果。

Conclusion: 该简化模型的严格计算结果可为更复杂、数学上难以处理的真实磁性介质模型提供有用的参考和理论基础。

Abstract: We consider a simple model of one-dimensional magnetic crystal and examine the propagation of an electromagnetic wave through such a medium. Calculating the dispersion relation ${\bf k}(ω)$ allows us to illustrate how the spread of the electromagnetic wave can be controlled by an external magnetic field. Our rigorous calculations should be useful for more realistic (and less tractable mathematically) models of magnetic media.

</details>


### [116] [Interplay of Valley, Orbital, Spin, and Layer Degrees of Freedom in Ta$_2$CS$_2$ MXene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01271)
*Kunal Dutta,Anupam Mondal,Sayantika Bhowal,Subhradip Ghosh,Indra Dasgupta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了非中心对称MXene材料Ta₂CS₂中谷、自旋、轨道和层等多重自由度的耦合特性，证明其可通过本征电极化调控，产生可切换的谷依赖轨道矩和类塞曼自旋分裂，并表现为轨道和自旋霍尔效应，为下一代自旋-轨道电子器件提供了平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维材料中多个自由度（谷、自旋、轨道、层）之间的耦合与调控机制，寻找可实现新型自旋-轨道电子器件的平台。

Method: 以MXene材料Ta₂CS₂为研究对象，通过理论计算分析其电子能带结构，研究自旋-轨道耦合下谷-轨道耦合和轨道-层耦合导致的谷依赖和层依赖的自旋分裂，以及本征电极化对多重自由度的调控作用。

Result: 发现Ta₂CS₂中存在谷-轨道耦合和轨道-层耦合；自旋-轨道耦合导致谷依赖和层依赖的自旋分裂；本征电极化可调控这些耦合自由度，实现可切换的谷依赖轨道矩和类塞曼自旋分裂；这些非平庸的轨道和自旋织构分别表现为轨道霍尔效应和自旋霍尔效应。

Conclusion: 非中心对称MXenes是探索多重自由度耦合、可调控性以及由此产生的轨道和自旋输运现象的 promising 平台，为下一代自旋-轨道电子器件铺平了道路。

Abstract: We show that the MXene Ta$_2$CS$_2$ provides an excellent platform for hosting multiple coupled degrees of freedom, viz., valley, spin, orbital, and layer. The interplay among these degrees of freedom gives rise to a range of intriguing properties in reciprocal space, including valley-orbital and orbital-layer coupling. In the presence of spin-orbit interaction, these couplings lead to valley-dependent and layer-dependent spin splitting of the electronic bands. We further show that the intrinsic electric polarization in Ta$_2$CS$_2$ introduces an additional tuning parameter, enabling control over these coupled degrees of freedom and resulting in switchable valley-dependent orbital moments and Zeeman-like spin splitting. We demonstrate that these nontrivial orbital and spin textures manifest in the orbital and spin Hall effects, respectively. Our results establish noncentrosymmetric MXenes as a promising platform for exploring the interplay among multiple degrees of freedom, their tunability, and the resulting orbital and spin transport phenomena in these two-dimensional materials, thereby paving the way for next-generation spin-orbitronic devices.

</details>


### [117] [Multi-probe detection of domain nucleation across the metal-insulator transition in VO$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01314)
*Shubhankar Paul,Giordano Mattoni,Amitava Ghosh,Pooja Kesarwani,Dipak Sahu,Monika Ahlawat,Ashok P,Amit Verma,Vishal Govind Rao,Chanchal Sow*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文以VO₂为模型材料，结合宏观（FORC）与微观（红外成像）多探针技术，系统研究了不同晶粒尺寸薄膜中金属-绝缘体相变（MIT）的畴分布与热滞现象，揭示了生长条件、畴相互作用与畴成核过程之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联体系中电子与结构自由度紧密耦合，理解相变过程中畴的分布与演化机制对揭示MIT的宏观与微观现象至关重要。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积（PLD）与直流溅射两种方法制备不同晶粒尺寸的VO₂薄膜，利用一阶反转曲线（FORC）进行宏观磁畴表征，结合红外成像进行微观畴分布成像，定量分析畴间相互作用。

Result: 揭示了FORC分布特征与热滞之间的关联，发现过冷金属畴与周围绝缘基质的相互作用主导了热滞行为，并建立了生长条件、畴相互作用与成核过程之间的定量相关性。

Conclusion: 多探针定量分析方法为理解强关联体系中MIT的畴动力学提供了有效手段，生长条件通过调控畴相互作用进而影响相变行为。

Abstract: Electronic and structural degrees of freedom are often intimately coupled in strongly correlated systems, which result in intriguing macroscopic and microscopic phenomena. Using the well-studied material VO$_2$ as a prototype, here we explore the domain distribution across the metal-insulator transition (MIT). We use macroscopic as well as microscopic techniques, such as first-order reversal curve (FORC) and infrared imaging, to probe the domain distributions across the MIT. This study compares MIT in thin films of VO$_2$ with different grain sizes grown by pulsed laser deposition and dc sputtering. We explore the relation between the nature of the FORC distribution and the corresponding thermal hysteresis due to interactions between the supercooled metallic domains and surrounding insulating matrix. Our multi-probe study with quantitative analysis provides a correlation between the growth, domain interaction, and domain nucleation process in MIT.

</details>


### [118] [Collinear ferromagnetism with reduced moment length in kagome magnet Nd3Ru4Al12](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01447)
*Yuki Ishihara,Ryota Nakano,Rinsuke Yamada,Takuya Nomoto,Priya R. Baral,Moritz M. Hirschmann,Kamini Gautam,Kamil K. Kolincio,Akiko Kikkawa,Seno Aji,Hiraku Saitoh,Masaaki Matsuda,Yasujiro Taguchi,Taka-hisa Arima,Yoshinori Tokura,Taro Nakajima,Max Hirschberger*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过单晶中子衍射和极化中子实验，确定了Nd3Ru4Al12的磁基态为共线铁磁态（hex-FM），所有Nd位点磁矩均匀（mc=2.1 μB/Nd），推翻了此前关于两个Nd位点磁矩不等的"ortho-FM"模型，为理解该材料在居里温度附近巨大的涨落诱导霍尔和能斯特效应提供了微观基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Nd3Ru4Al12这一kagome晶格磁体的磁基态，解决此前文献中关于其磁有序模型（"ortho-FM" vs "hex-FM"）的矛盾，为解释其异常的热电输运现象提供正确的微观磁结构基础。

Method: 采用单晶中子衍射技术确定磁基态，辅以极化中子实验测量翻转比（flipping ratio）进行验证和区分不同磁结构模型。

Result: 1. Nd3Ru4Al12的磁基态为共线铁磁态（hex-FM），磁序波矢Q=0；2. 所有Nd位点磁矩长度均匀，mc=2.1 μB/Nd；3. 极化中子散射的翻转比分析一致支持hex-FM态，排除了此前提出的"ortho-FM"不等磁矩模型。

Conclusion: 该研究修正了Nd3Ru4Al12的磁结构模型，指出其基态为共线铁磁体而非不等磁矩的复杂铁磁态，为理解该材料在TC=41 K附近巨大的涨落诱导霍尔效应和能斯特效应提供了正确的微观磁图像。

Abstract: We determine the magnetic ground state of the kagome lattice magnet Nd3Ru4Al12 by single-crystal neutron diffraction, supported by experiments with polarized neutrons. We identify this material as a collinear ferromagnet ("hex-FM") with uniform moment length mc = 2.1 μB/Nd and ordering vector Q = 0, in contrast to a previous, seminal report that proposed unequal moment lengths on two Nd sites, here called the "ortho-FM" state. Our analysis of the flipping ratio in polarized neutron scattering is consistent with the hex-FM state. The results provide a microscopic basis for understanding the large fluctuation-induced Hall and Nernst responses near TC = 41 K, as previously reported for Nd3Ru4Al12.

</details>


### [119] [Topological Ising superconductivity in two-dimensional p-wave magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01686)
*Kyoung-Min Kim,Gibaik Sim,Moon Jip Park*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究方形晶格p波磁体中的超导不稳定性，发现奇宇称交换场允许自旋单态和三重态混合，导致s+p_x Ising态，并可调控进入具有马约拉纳边缘态的拓扑超导相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非相对论交换场引起的费米面自旋劈裂作为实现拓扑超导电性的新途径，避免依赖强自旋轨道耦合。

Method: 对方形晶格p波磁体，考虑格点和最近邻吸引相互作用，研究超导不稳定性，分析不同对称性通道中的序参量混合及拓扑性质。

Result: 奇宇称交换场允许单态和三重态在A1对称性通道混合；主导不稳定性为可调谐的s+p_x Ising态；当三重态超过单态时进入拓扑超导相，具有受动量分辨绕数保护的马约拉纳边缘模；垂直塞曼场可诱导Z2拓扑超导相。

Conclusion: p波磁体中的交换场为实现可调控的拓扑超导电性提供了新平台，无需强自旋轨道耦合。

Abstract: Fermi-surface spin splitting generated by non-relativistic exchange fields provides a new route to topological superconductivity without relying on strong spin-orbit coupling. Here, we study superconducting instabilities of a square-lattice $p$-wave magnet with onsite and nearest-neighbour attractive interactions. The odd-parity exchange field removes inversion symmetry in the spin-split electronic structure, allowing singlet and triplet order parameters to mix within a single $A_1$ symmetry channel. The leading instability is a coupled $s+p_x$ Ising state, whose singlet-triplet balance is continuously tunable by the relative strength of the nearest-neighbour attraction. When the triplet gap amplitude exceeds the singlet one, this Ising state undergoes a transition into a nodal topological superconducting phase with Majorana edge modes protected by momentum-resolved winding numbers. These modes extend over finite momentum intervals bounded by the surface projections of bulk point nodes. We further show that a Zeeman field perpendicular to the exchange field can induce a $Z_2$ topological superconducting phase, even in the regime where a single gap dominates.

</details>


### [120] [Lattice Realization of Twist Defects in a $\mathbb{Z}_2\times \mathbb{Z}_2$ Topological Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02039)
*Gustavo M. Yoshitome*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在$\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$拓扑序（双环面码）中实现了三种任意子对称性，通过堆叠两个解耦的Wen plaquette模型并引入位错缺陷，诱导层间相互作用。缺陷表现为分支割裂源，携带非平凡任意子置换。通过计算量子维度和双环算子，确定了缺陷的非阿贝尔融合规则，包括携带不同任意子置换的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拓扑序系统中实现和表征不同类型的任意子对称性（即非平凡置换任意子标签的对称性），并研究由此产生的缺陷及其非阿贝尔特性。

Method: 采用两个解耦的Wen plaquette模型堆叠，通过引入位错修改哈密顿量，产生局域扭曲缺陷并诱导层间相互作用。通过计算量子维度表征缺陷，并构造双环算子来确定缺陷的非阿贝尔融合规则。

Result: 成功在$\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$拓扑序中实现了三种任意子对称性；缺陷携带不同的任意子置换；计算得到了缺陷的量子维度和非阿贝尔融合规则。

Conclusion: 该研究为任意子对称性的微观实现提供了具体框架，揭示了拓扑序中缺陷诱导的任意子置换及其非阿贝尔融合特性。

Abstract: In this work, we explore a microscopic realization of three types of anyonic symmetries in a $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological order, corresponding to a double toric code. These symmetries act as nontrivial permutations on the anyon labels of the parent state. We consider a setup consisting of two decoupled Wen plaquette models stacked on top of each other and introduce dislocations that modify the Hamiltonian, giving rise to localized twist defects, eventually inducing interactions between the layers. In this context, branch cuts act as sources of anyon permutations when they cross it. We characterize the defects by calculating their quantum dimensions, and we also consider double loop operators around them that allow us to determine the non-Abelian fusion rules between the defects, including when they carry different anyon permutations.

</details>


### [121] [Validity and Limits of Low Order Hybridization Expansion Approaches for Multi-Orbital Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02228)
*Dolev Goldberger,Ido Zemach,Lei Zhang,Yang Yu,Emanuel Gull,Guy Cohen,André Erpenbeck*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了低阶 hybridization 展开方法（NCA 和 OCA）在多轨道杂质问题中的准确性，发现这些方法的精度受限于最弱关联的轨道，该轨道的快速衰减特性会通过截断展开产生的虚假耦合传播到其他轨道，抑制如近藤共振等关联效应。通过解析推导和数值验证，提供了低阶方法在多轨道体系中成功与失败参数范围的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低阶 hybridization 展开方法（NCA 和 OCA）是强关联系统中广泛使用的杂质求解器，但它们在真实多轨道环境下的准确性尚不清楚。本文旨在系统性地评估这些方法在多轨道体系中的表现，揭示其失效机制，并确定其适用范围。

Method: 以解耦轨道极限作为受控参考点，推导多轨道限制传播子和格林函数与其单轨道对应物之间的解析关系；识别导致低阶方法在多轨道中失效的图论机制；通过代表性两轨道模型系统的稳态数值计算进行验证。

Result: 低阶方法的准确性由最弱关联的轨道（即具有最快衰减延迟格林函数的轨道）决定；该轨道的性质通过截断展开产生的虚假耦合传播到所有其他轨道，抑制了如近藤共振等关联特征；即使在单轨道计算中表现为强关联的轨道，在多轨道环境下也会受到影响。

Conclusion: 本文提供了评估单轨道计算结果能否推广到多轨道体系的实用指南，并为开发和验证高阶多轨道杂质求解器提供了基准。

Abstract: Low-order hybridization expansion methods such as the non-crossing approximation (NCA) and the one-crossing approximation (OCA) are widely used impurity solvers in the study of strongly correlated systems, yet their accuracy in genuine multi-orbital settings remains poorly understood. Using the decoupled orbital limit as a controlled reference point, we derive analytic results connecting multi-orbital restricted propagators and Green's functions to their single-orbital counterparts, identify the diagrammatic mechanisms responsible for the breakdown of low-order methods in multi-orbital settings, and determine their regimes of applicability. Our central finding is that the accuracy of these methods is governed by the least correlated orbital: i.e., the orbital with the most rapidly decaying retarded Green's function. That orbital's properties are transferred to all other orbitals through a spurious coupling generated by the truncated expansion, thereby suppressing correlation-induced features such as the Kondo resonance. This occurs even in orbitals that are themselves strongly correlated within single-orbital calculations using the same approximation scheme. We confirm this numerically across representative two-orbital model systems in the steady-state, systematically identifying the parameter regimes in which low-order methods succeed or fail. Our results provide a practical guide for assessing when insights from single-orbital calculations carry over to multi-orbital settings, and serve as a benchmark for the development and validation of higher-order multi-orbital impurity solvers.

</details>


### [122] [First-Principles Effective Mass in the Three-Dimensional Uniform Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02294)
*Pengcheng Hou,Daniel Cerkoney,Zhiyi Li,Tao Wang,Xiansheng Cai,Lei Wang,Gabriel Kotliar,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过重正化微扰理论首次在金属密度区间（r_s ≤ 6）从第一性原理出发，通过自能和前向散射四点顶点两条互补路径一致地确定了三维均匀电子气的准粒子有效质量m*/m，发现其值始终接近1，呈现浅的非单调密度依赖（r_s≈1附近极小值后微弱上升），驳斥了强单调抑制的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维均匀电子气的准粒子有效质量m*是一个基础费米液体参数，但其数值和密度依赖关系几十年来一直存在争议，需要从第一性原理给出明确结论。

Method: 采用带有显式抵消项的重正化微扰理论，通过自能和前向散射四点顶点（经由p波自旋对称朗道参数F₁ˢ）两条互补路径，在第六重正化阶次下一致地计算m*。

Result: m*/m在整个金属区间（r_s≤6）始终接近1，呈现浅的非单调密度依赖——在r_s≈1附近出现极小值后缓慢上升，不支持强单调抑制行为。

Conclusion: 金属UEG中主导的电荷关联集中在前向散射区域，仅产生微弱的F₁ˢ分量，这与观测到的有效质量行为一致。

Abstract: The quasiparticle effective mass $m^*$ of the three-dimensional uniform electron gas (UEG) is a fundamental Fermi-liquid parameter whose value and density dependence have remained controversial for decades. Using renormalized perturbation theory with explicit counterterms, we determine $m^*$ in the metallic regime ($r_s \le 6$) from first principles by two complementary routes -- the self-energy and the forward-scattering four-point vertex via the $p$-wave spin-symmetric Landau parameter $F_1^s$ -- that agree within uncertainties at each density through sixth renormalized order. The resulting $m^*/m$ remains close to unity throughout the metallic regime, with a shallow non-monotonic density dependence -- a minimum near $r_s\approx 1$ followed by a gentle upturn -- reflecting the interplay of exchange and dynamical screening in the self-energy, and disfavoring strong monotonic suppression. This finding supports a physical picture for the metallic UEG in which dominant charge correlations are concentrated in nearly forward scattering and generate only a weak $F_1^s$ component.

</details>


### [123] [Exchange-frustrated quadrupoles on the honeycomb lattice: Flavor-wave spectra, classical degeneracies and parton constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02336)
*Partha Sarker,Han Ma,Urban F. P. Seifert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了$S=1$蜂窝晶格上的四极子Kitaev模型，通过多种互补方法揭示了其丰富的量子无序相结构，包括经典基态简并、有效低能哈密顿量、$\mathbb Z_2$规范结构以及不同Majorana平均场方案下的量子无序态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索$S \geq 1$ Kitaev材料中受挫四极子相互作用能否诱导量子无序相，为相关材料体系（如Rydberg阵列量子模拟器）提供理论依据。

Method: 综合运用半经典变分分析（基于SU(3)味理论）、微扰理论（围绕键各向异性极限）、Majorana parton构造（揭示$\mathbb Z_2$规范结构并构建不同平均场ansätze）等多种互补方法。

Result: 发现了经典平均场基态的高度简并流形；键各向异性极限下有效低能哈密顿量依赖于残余对称性$\mathcal{M}$；Majorana parton构造揭示了规范电荷凝聚驱动的禁闭/退禁闭相；不同Majorana平均场ansätze产生了有能隙和无能隙的量子无序候选态，两者通过$\mathcal{M}$的线性或投影实现区分。

Conclusion: 受挫四极子相互作用是通往量子无序相的有效途径，对$S \geq 1$ Kitaev材料及Rydberg阵列量子模拟器具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We study the quadrupolar Kitaev model, an $S=1$ honeycomb-lattice model with frustrated bond-dependent quadrupolar interactions. Using complementary methods and expanding around controlled limits, we uncover several intertwined structures. First, a semiclassical variational analysis based on $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ flavor theory reveals an extensively degenerate manifold of classical mean-field ground states, suggesting that quantum fluctuations may stabilize a quantum-disordered phase. Second, in the bond-anisotropic limit, perturbation theory is used to derive effective low-energy Hamiltonians, which crucially depend on the presence (or absence) of a residual symmetry $\mathcal{M}$ of combined lattice reflection and discrete spin rotation. A Majorana parton construction uncovers an exact $\mathbb Z_2$ gauge structure and motivates possible confined and deconfined phases driven by gauge-charge condensation, consistent with the effective theories obtained in anisotropic limit. Further, within the same parton formalism, different Majorana mean-field ansätze produce both gapless and gapped candidate quantum-disordered states, distinguished by linear versus projective implementations of $\mathcal M$. Our results highlight frustrated quadrupolar interactions as a route to quantum-disordered phases, relevant to $S \geq 1$ Kitaev materials and Rydberg-array quantum simulators.

</details>


### [124] [Entanglement signature of fully and partially dimerized phases in frustrated spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02581)
*Wuttichai Pankeaw,Teparksorn Pengpan,Pruet Kalasuwan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种阻挫自旋链（自旋-1/2 Majumdar-Ghosh模型和自旋-3/2 J1-J2-J3链）中价键基态的von Neumann纠缠熵，发现所有情况下纠缠熵在热力学极限下均饱和为有限常数，证实了面积律行为，并揭示了不同二聚化相中纠缠熵可作为阻挫键架构的稳健指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索阻挫一维自旋链中精确价键基态的纠缠熵行为，并验证纠缠熵能否作为区分不同二聚化相（不同自旋大小、键多重性和二聚化模式）的稳健特征。

Method: 利用矩阵乘积态表示，在开放和周期边界条件下，对三种互补二分划（半链、单格点、配对）计算纠缠熵随系统尺寸的变化，并通过有限尺寸标度提取饱和值。

Result: 所有情况下纠缠熵在热力学极限下均饱和为有限常数，符合面积律；饱和值与虚拟自旋键结构直接相关。MG模型和完全二聚化相表现出相似的纠缠行为，而部分二聚化相呈现定性不同的特征，包括依赖于切口键类型的多重半链饱和值、单格点熵中的不对称边缘贡献以及配对熵中的多能带结构。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵可作为阻挫键架构的稳健指纹，能够清晰区分具有不同自旋大小、键多重性和二聚化模式的各二聚化相。

Abstract: The von Neumann entanglement entropy of exact valence-bond ground states is studied in two frustrated one-dimensional spin chains: the spin-1/2 Majumdar-Ghosh (MG) model and the spin-3/2 J1-J2-J3 chain in its fully dimerized (FD) and partially dimerized (PD) phases. Using matrix-product-state representations, the entropy is computed as a function of system size for three complementary bipartitions - half-chain, single-site, and pairwise - under both open and periodic boundary conditions. In all cases, the entropy saturates to a finite constant in the thermodynamic limit, confirming area-law behavior. The saturation values, extracted via finite-size scaling, are directly related to the underlying virtual-spin bond structure. The MG model and FD phase exhibit similar entanglement behavior, differing primarily in saturation magnitude determined by spin value and bond multiplicity, and both display even-odd oscillations and exponential convergence with system size. In contrast, the PD phase shows qualitatively distinct signatures, including multiple half-chain saturation values depending on the bond type at the cut, asymmetric edge contributions in the single-site entropy, and a multi-band structure in the pairwise entropy reflecting the coexistence of single- and double-singlet bonds. These results establish entanglement entropy as a robust signature of frustrated bond architecture, enabling clear distinction among dimerized phases with different spin magnitude, bond multiplicity, and dimerization patterns.

</details>


### [125] [NESSi 2.0: The Non-Equilibrium Systems Simulation package version 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02606)
*Fabian Künzel,Michael Schüler,Denis Golež,Yuta Murakami,Sujay Ray,Christopher Stahl,Jiajun Li,Hugo U. R. Strand,Philipp Werner,Martin Eckstein*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文扩展了非平衡格林函数模拟软件包NESSi，引入了内存截断技术和非平衡稳态描述功能，将计算复杂度从O(N_t^3)降低到O(N_t N_c^2)，内存需求从O(N_t^2)降低到O(N_c^2)，从而支持更长时间尺度的量子多体动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡格林函数模拟在时间跨度大时（从亚飞秒电子动力学到皮秒集体模动力学）面临计算瓶颈，Kadanoff-Baym方程的内存积分导致计算成本O(N_t^3)和内存需求O(N_t^2)随时间步数快速增长，限制了模拟可达的时间尺度。

Method: (i) 内存截断：将KBE中的内存积分截断至最多N_c个时间步，计算复杂度降为O(N_t N_c^2)，内存需求降为O(N_c^2)；(ii) 引入非平衡稳态描述功能，适用于时间平移不变的输运系统及缓慢演化的预平衡态。

Result: 内存截断技术在结果收敛于截断参数N_c的前提下，可显著扩展模拟至更长时间；非平衡稳态功能可有效描述输运体系及预平衡态。

Conclusion: 该扩展有效突破了非平衡格林函数模拟的时间尺度瓶颈，为研究超快激光驱动固体中的量子多体动力学提供了更高效的数值工具。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium Green's functions provide a powerful framework for studying quantum many-body dynamics including the laser-induced dynamics in solids. The Non-Equilibrium Systems Simulation package (NESSi) offers an efficient platform for such simulations, ranging from perturbative approaches like nonequilibrium $GW$ to nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory. However, simulations based on nonequilibrium Green's functions become computationally demanding when the dynamics span a large temporal range, such as from sub-femtosecond electron dynamics to the picosecond dynamics of collective modes. Due to the memory integral in the Kadanoff-Baym equations, which serve as equations of motion for nonequilibrium Green's functions, the computational cost scales as $\mathcal{O}(N_t^3)$ with the number of timesteps $N_t$, and the memory requirement scales as $\mathcal{O}(N_t^2)$. In this work, we extend NESSi by incorporating techniques that aim to overcome this bottleneck: (i) By truncating the memory integrals in the KBE to a maximum of $N_c$ timesteps, the computational complexity is reduced to $\mathcal{O}(N_tN_c^2)$, and the memory requirement to $\mathcal{O}(N_c^2)$. Provided that the results converge with respect to the cutoff $N_c$, memory truncation allows to extend the simulations to significantly longer times. (ii) We introduce functionalities to describe nonequilibrium steady states, i.e. time-translationally invariant nonequilibrium states. Such states are relevant for transport settings, and they provide an approximate description of slowly evolving (prethermal) nonequilibrium states.

</details>


### [126] [The flow of local quantum fluids: Conservation laws and vertex corrections from many-body linear-response theory with local self-energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02625)
*Davide Valentinis*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在局域、频率依赖相互作用及电荷/质量守恒假设下，严格推导了强关联量子电子系统在非扩散传导区间的非局域电动力学响应。通过Bethe-Salpeter方程处理重整化顶点修正，证明了在特定对称性条件下（如反演对称、镜像反射、旋转对称等）顶点修正可严格为零，并给出了费米液体与非费米液体的通用非局域关联函数表达式，讨论了对称性破缺、多能带效应及动量守恒的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强关联量子电子系统的非扩散传导区间，电磁扰动同时探测电子动力学的时间（频率）和空间（波矢）行为，需要严格处理非局域响应。现有理论框架中顶点修正的精确行为尚不清晰，亟需建立一般性准则来简化计算并揭示普适规律。

Method: 基于Bethe-Salpeter方程重整化相互作用顶点，结合Kubo形式论处理双粒子关联函数（密度、电流、动量、应力），在局域频率依赖相互作用及电荷/质量守恒假设下，分析顶点修正的对称性约束条件，并推广至多能带、动量守恒及Landau能级Hall黏度等情形。

Result: 1. q=0时，若单粒子色散具有反演对称性且裸顶点在特定点群变换下为奇函数（矢量顶点对反演，张量顶点对镜像反射或二/更高重旋转），则顶点修正严格为零。2. 二次型色散下，电流-电流关联函数中顶点修正对任意q和ω恒为零。3. 给出了费米液体（有准粒子峰）和非费米液体（无准粒子）的通用非局域关联函数解析表达式。

Conclusion: 建立了局域相互作用量子流体中非局域电动力学响应的严格框架，揭示了顶点修正为零的普适对称性条件，为强关联体系（包括非费米液体）的非局域输运和光学响应计算提供了简化工具。

Abstract: In non-diffusive conduction regimes of strongly correlated quantum electron systems, electromagnetic perturbations simultaneously probe the electronic dynamics in time and space: the exchanged energy $\hbar ω$ excites retarded, i.e., frequency-dependent, many-body interactions, while the probing spatial modulation renders the response spatially nonlocal, i.e., dependent on the external wave vector $\vec{q}$. This work determines the exact nonlocal electrodynamic response of such dynamical quantum fluids under the assumptions of local, frequency-dependent interactions and charge/mass conservation. The latter is ensured by Bethe-Salpeter equations for renormalized interaction vertices, entering the Kubo formalism for two-particle correlation functions (e.g., for density, currents, momentum, stress). Within such framework, it is shown that vertex corrections generally vanish at $q=0$ for single-particle dispersions with inversion symmetry and for bare interaction vertices that are odd with respect to specific point group transformations in momentum space, including inversion for vector vertices, and mirror reflections or two- or higher-fold rotations for tensor vertices. In addition, for quadratic dispersion vertex corrections identically vanish from the current-current correlation function, at any momentum $\vec{q}$ and frequency $ω$. The robustness of these criteria against further symmetry breaking, multiband effects, and additionally imposing momentum conservation, is discussed, with application to the Hall viscosity of Landau levels. Explicit expressions for generic nonlocal correlation functions are derived for Fermi liquids (with well-defined quasiparticle peaks) and non-Fermi liquids (devoid of quasiparticles), for arbitrary local self-energies.

</details>


### [127] [Benchmarking the Dual Fermion approach on the Falicov-Kimball model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02717)
*Akshat Mishra,Hugo U. R. Strand,Erik G. C. P. van Loon*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过Falicov-Kimball模型的精确经典蒙特卡洛解，基准测试了梯形Dual Fermion方法和动力学平均场理论（DMFT）。研究发现Dual Fermion在热力学、电子结构和磁化率方面明显优于DMFT，但在掺杂体系中轨道密度与化学势关系的描述上精度不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dual Fermion方法基于物理动机但原则上不受控的近似，需要谨慎的基准测试以理解其优势和局限性。

Method: 以Falicov-Kimball模型为测试平台，将梯形Dual Fermion和DMFT的结果与精确经典蒙特卡洛解进行对比，重点分析热力学性质、电子结构以及联合频率-动量空间的磁化率。

Result: Dual Fermion在热力学、电子结构和磁化率（尤其是频率-动量联合结构）方面显著优于DMFT；但在描述掺杂体系中轨道密度与化学势的关系时精度不足。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，对包含不等价轨道的模型进行严格基准测试是必要的，这对于材料模拟至关重要。

Abstract: Strong electronic correlations generally require non-perturbative treatment. Local correlations are captured by dynamical mean-field theory while nonlocal correlations can be treated with diagrammatic extensions such as the Dual Fermion approach. Dual Fermion is built on physically motivated, but in principle uncontrolled approximations, so careful benchmarking is needed to understand the strengths and limitations of the method. In this work, we benchmark ladder Dual Fermion and dynamical mean-field theory for the Falicov-Kimball model with the exact classical Monte Carlo solution. We focus on the thermodynamics, electronic structure and susceptibility, especially at the combined frequency and momentum structure, and find that Dual Fermion clearly outperforms dynamical mean-field theory. Somewhat surprisingly, Dual Fermion is not as accurate for the relation between orbital density versus chemical potential in the doped system. These results demonstrate the need for rigorous benchmarking of diagrammatic extensions of dynamical mean-field theory for models with inequivalent orbitals, which is essential for modelling materials.

</details>


### [128] [Interlayer Five-Spin Polaron in Superconducting Bilayer Nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02891)
*Jiarui Li,Christopher T. Parzyck,Eder G. Lomeli,Yidi Liu,Taehun Kim,Heemin Lee,Zengqing Zhuo,Eun Kyo Ko,Yaoju Tarn,Cheng-Tai Kuo,Ronny Sutarto,Chunjing Jia,Vivek Thampy,Brian Moritz,Yijun Yu,Jun-Sik Lee,Valentina Bisogni,Thomas P. Devereaux,Harold Y. Hwang,Wei-Sheng Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过共振X射线散射研究证实，双层镍氧化物超导体La₂PrNi₂O₇中，超导性与自旋密度波（SDW）序呈相分离状态：超导发生在无SDW的氧化学计量区域，而氧空位则诱导SDW序。结合光谱学实验与理论，提出配体空穴主要占据层间顶点氧位置，形成层间五自旋极化子态，作为超导基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双层镍氧化物中磁性基态与多轨道物理的相互作用，揭示超导配对机制，特别是自旋密度波（SDW）序与超导配对之间的关系。

Method: 利用共振X射线散射技术检测超导双层镍氧化物薄膜La₂PrNi₂O₇中的SDW序；对比超导与缺氧薄膜的电子结构（Ni-L₃和O-K边光谱）；结合理论分析配体空穴的分布。

Result: 超导发生在无SDW的氧化学计量区域，缺氧区域则出现SDW序，两者呈相分离；超导与缺氧区域在c轴方向的电子结构存在显著差异；氧化学计量是调控层间耦合及电子结构的关键参数。

Conclusion: 配体空穴主要位于层间顶点氧上，形成稳健的层间五自旋极化子态，作为超导双层镍氧化物的基态。

Abstract: The discovery of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates has sparked substantial effort towards understanding unconventional electronic states beyond a traditional cuprate-like d^9 configurational ground state. An understanding of the interplay between magnetic ground states and multi-orbital physics is key for establishing a microscopic mechanism for superconductivity. In the bilayer nickelates, spin density wave (SDW) order is a prominent feature in the non-superconducting regime. However, its relation to superconducting pairing remains an open question. Here, we use resonant x-ray scattering to examine the existence of SDW order in superconducting bilayer nickelate thin films La$_2$PrNi$_2$O$_7$ (LPNO). Comparing superconducting and oxygen-deficient LPNO thin films, we find that superconductivity occurs in SDW-free, oxygen-stoichiometric regions, whereas oxygen-deficiency promotes SDW order, indicating phase segregation of SDW and superconductivity. Furthermore, Ni-$L_3$ and O-$K$ edge spectroscopy reveals distinct electronic structures - particularly along the $c$-axis - between the two domains. Our results identify oxygen stoichiometry as a key parameter controlling interlayer coupling and thus the electronic structure of bilayer nickelates. In concert with theory, we propose that a ligand hole primarily resides at the inter-bilayer apical oxygen, forming a robust interlayer five-spin polaron state, which serves as the ground state for superconducting bilayer nickelates.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了强相互作用里德伯原子阵列中的希尔伯特空间碎片化现象，发现碎片维度随系统规模呈现不同标度行为，并通过辅助费米子描述揭示了其背后的动力学约束机制，为超越PXP模型的非遍历多体动力学工程提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究里德伯原子阵列中希尔伯特空间碎片化的存在条件及其对非遍历动力学的影响，揭示碎片化背后的动力学约束机制。

Method: 通过改变全局里德伯耦合的失谐量，分析碎片维度随系统规模的标度行为；采用辅助费米子描述揭示碎片化与碎片约束动力学背后的涌现动力学约束。

Result: 希尔伯特子空间的存在依赖于失谐与相互作用的博弈，且呈现强碎片化特征，碎片维度随系统规模表现出多种标度行为；系统的约束动力学由碎片的维度和连通性决定。

Conclusion: 研究为里德伯阵列中的希尔伯特空间碎片化提供了系统性理解，并为超越PXP模型工程化非遍历多体动力学奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In a strongly interacting Rydberg atom array, the dynamics are often constrained to the decoupled Hilbert subspaces, representing an intriguing paradigm for nonergodicity. By considering a variable detuning of the global Rydberg coupling, we show that, not only is the existence of these Hilbert subspaces dependent on the interplay of detuning and interaction, but they are also strongly fragmented, with the fragment dimensions exhibiting various scaling behaviors with increasing system size. The resulting constrained dynamics of the system are thus governed by the dimension and connectivity of these fragments. We then adopt an auxiliary fermion description to reveal the underlying emergent kinetic constraints for the subspace fragmentation and fragment-confined dynamics. Our results provide a systematic understanding of Hilbert-space fragmentation in Rydberg arrays, and shed light on engineering nonergodic many-body dynamics beyond the PXP model.

</details>


### [130] [Enhancing supercurrent-based inertial sensing via interactions in atomtronic angular accelerometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02048)
*S. Carmona-López,A. Matos-Abiague,F. Isaule,L. Morales-Molina*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了在外部旋转的角向交流 shaken 环状晶格中的超冷原子超流，展示了如何利用这些超流开发高精度原子角加速度计。研究发现，当驱动频率调谐至 Bloch 频率的整数分之一时产生显著净原子流；在单粒子区，共振宽度与平均时间成反比，决定了傅里叶极限灵敏度；而引入弱相互作用可突破这一极限，灵敏度提高至少两个数量级，优于先前提出的基于超冷原子的角加速度计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用超冷原子在角向交流驱动的环状晶格中产生的超流来开发高精度原子角加速度计，并研究相互作用对灵敏度的影响。

Method: 采用理论分析与数值模拟相结合的方法，在 Bose-Hubbard 模型框架内，研究超冷原子在经受外部旋转的角向交流 shaken 环状晶格中的超流行为。

Result: 驱动频率调谐至 Bloch 频率整数分之一时产生显著净原子流，远离共振时平均电流近乎为零；单粒子区灵敏度受傅里叶极限约束；弱相互作用可突破该极限，灵敏度提升至少两个数量级。

Conclusion: 超冷原子超流可用于开发高精度原子角加速度计，且弱相互作用可大幅提升传感器灵敏度，为基于原子流的新型惯性传感器开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate supercurrents of ultracold atoms in angularly ac-shaken ring lattices subjected to external rotation. Our results demonstrate how these supercurrents can be harnessed for the development of high-precision atomtronic angular accelerometers. Using both analytical and numerical approaches within the Bose-Hubbard model framework, we demonstrate that a significant net atomic current arises when the lattice driving frequency is tuned to an integer fraction of the Bloch frequency, while the current averages to nearly zero away from such a resonance. In the single-particle regime, the resonance width scales inversely with the averaging time, thereby setting a fundamental Fourier-limited bound on the measurement's sensitivity. Strikingly, our numerical simulations demonstrate that this Fourier limit - a fundamental barrier in the non-interacting system - can be surpassed by introducing weak interactions between atoms. In the interacting regime, the sensitivity surpasses the Fourier-limited scaling with the averaging time, achieving an improvement of at least two orders of magnitude over the single-particle scenario, and exceeding the performance of previously proposed ultracold-atom-based angular accelerometers. These findings pave the way for developing new atomic-current-based inertial sensors with interaction-enhanced sensitivity.

</details>


### [131] [Dynamical universality in a driven quantum fluid of light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02397)
*Ivan Gnusov,Paolo Comaron,Antonio Gianfrate,Dimitrios Trypogeorgos,Marzena Szymanska,Paolo Cazzato,Milena De Giorgi,Daniele Sanvitto,Dario Ballarini*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过实验测量了半导体微腔中激子-极化激元驱动量子光流体的临界动力学行为，发现关联长度ξ与弛豫时间τ满足普适标度关系τ ∝ ξ^z，动力学指数z≈2，揭示了非守恒序参量的扩散动力学，将平衡态临界动力学拓展至驱动光学系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相变附近的普适标度是物理学核心概念，但此前直接实验观测主要局限于平衡态多体系统的静态临界行为，缺乏对驱动非平衡系统中临界动力学的直接实验测量。

Method: 在半导体微腔中构建激子-极化激元驱动的量子光流体，在凝聚阈值以下的涨落主导无序相中，直接测量关联长度ξ的静态增长和弛豫时间τ的动态减慢。

Result: ξ与τ满足普适关系τ ∝ ξ^z，动力学指数z≈2，对应非守恒序参量的扩散型动力学。

Conclusion: 本研究将临界动力学物理从平衡态物质扩展至驱动光学系统，桥接了量子凝聚体和激光器两类系统。

Abstract: Universal scaling near phase transitions is one of the central ideas of physics, linking the growth of spatial correlations to the slowing down of dynamics. So far, direct experimental access to this critical behavior has remained largely confined to equilibrium many-body systems, and especially to static critical behavior. Here we probe how universality emerges in a driven quantum fluid of light formed by exciton--polaritons in a semiconductor microcavity. By probing the fluctuation-dominated disordered phase below the condensation threshold, we directly measure both the static growth of the correlation length $ξ$ and the dynamical slowing down of the relaxation time $τ$. We find that these quantities obey the universal relation $τ\propto ξ^{z}$ with dynamical exponent $z \approx 2$, revealing diffusive dynamics of a non-conserved order parameter. Our results extend the physics of critical dynamics from equilibrium matter to driven optical systems, bridging quantum condensates and lasers.

</details>
